## `AGO-2733-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Temperature-Rise Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Wiring Device Micro-Ohmmeter Contact Resistance & Temp-Rise
